Lemma 17

Suppose that
\sigma:\mathbb{F}\to\mathbb{F}
satisfies
2
.
Assume that there exists an integer
e_{0}\in\mathbb{Z}
such that
2^{e_{0}}\leq\left|\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)\right|<2^{e_{0}+1}

and define
\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}
as

\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}\coloneq\max\left({\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M,-e_{0}+\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1}\right).

Suppose that
\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}\leq-3
,
e_{0}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}-M-3-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}
.

If
\sigma(\eta)<\sigma(\eta^{+})
, define
\theta,\mathcal{I},\mathcal{I}^{+}
as

\theta\coloneq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}},\mathcal{I}\coloneq\langle\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}},\sigma(\eta)\rangle,\text{ and }\mathcal{I}^{+}\coloneq\langle\sigma(\eta^{+}),\sigma(\eta)+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\rangle,

and if
\sigma(\eta)>\sigma(\eta^{+})
, define
\theta,\mathcal{I},\mathcal{I}^{+}
as

\theta\coloneq-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}},\mathcal{I}^{+}\coloneq\langle\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}},\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)\rangle,\text{ and }\mathcal{I}\coloneq\langle\sigma(\eta),\sigma(\eta)+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\rangle,

where

\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\coloneq\begin{cases}\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}-M-1&\text{ if }\eta>0\;\text{or}\;\eta<0,\eta\neq-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}},\\
\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}-M-2&\text{ if }\eta<0,\eta=-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}}.\end{cases}

Then, there exists
k\in\mathbb{N}
and
\alpha_{1},\dots,\alpha_{k},z_{1},\dots,z_{k}\in\mathbb{F}
such that for

\displaystyle f(x)=(\theta\otimes x)\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.02228pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{k}(\alpha_{i}\otimes\sigma(z_{i}))\oplus\eta,

the followings hold:

\displaystyle f^{\sharp}(\langle-\Omega,\Omega\rangle)\subset\langle-\Omega,\Omega\rangle,\quad f^{\sharp}(\mathcal{I})=\langle\eta,\eta\rangle,\quad f^{\sharp}(\mathcal{I}^{+})=\langle\eta^{+},\eta^{+}\rangle.

(62)

In addition, if
\sigma(\eta)<\sigma(\eta^{+})

\displaystyle f(x)-\eta^{+}\leq\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}+2}\quad

\displaystyle\text{for}\quad\sigma(\eta)+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\leq x\in\mathbb{F},

(63)

\displaystyle\eta-f(x)\leq\left({\sigma\left({\eta}\right)-x}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}+2}\quad

\displaystyle\text{for}\quad\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\geq x\in\mathbb{F},

(64)

and if

\sigma(\eta)>\sigma(\eta^{+})
, we have

\displaystyle\eta-f(x)\leq\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}+2}\quad\quad

\displaystyle\text{for}\quad\sigma(\eta)+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\leq x\in\mathbb{F},

(65)

\displaystyle f(x)-\eta^{+}\leq\left({\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)-x}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}+2}\quad

\displaystyle\text{for}\quad\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\geq x\in\mathbb{F},

(66)

Proof.

First,
\mathcal{I}^{+}
is not empty
because
|\sigma(\eta^{+})-\sigma(\eta)|<2^{e_{0}+1}\leq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}-M-2-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\leq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}
.
Since
f
is monotone, we only need to consider the endpoints of
\mathcal{I}
and
\mathcal{I}^{+}
.

Note that
2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1}\in\mathbb{F}
(since
\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}\geq 5+\mathfrak{e}_{\min}
)
and the following hold:

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1}

\displaystyle=\eta^{+}-\eta,\;\left({1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1}}\right)^{-}\oplus\eta=\eta^{+}.

(67)

Now, represent
\eta
as

\eta=\mathfrak{s}_{\eta}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}}=1.\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,1}\dots\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}}.

Then we have if
\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0
, we have

\displaystyle\eta\oplus 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}=\eta,\;\eta\oplus(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})=\eta,\;\eta\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}=\eta^{+},\;\eta\oplus\left(-\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}\right)=\eta^{-},

(68)

and if
\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1
, we have

\displaystyle\eta\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{-}=\eta,\;\eta\oplus\left(-\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{-}\right)=\eta,\;\eta\oplus 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}=\eta^{+},\;\eta\oplus(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})=\eta^{-}.

(69)

By
Lemma

16
, there exists
n\in\mathbb{N}
,
i\in[n]
,
\widetilde{\alpha_{i}}\in\mathbb{F}
such that for
g:\mathbb{F}\rightarrow\mathbb{F}
defined as

g(x)\coloneq x\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{n}\left({\widetilde{\alpha_{i}}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right),

one of the following statements holds:

g^{\sharp}\left({\langle-1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},0\rangle}\right)\subset\langle-(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-},(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-}\rangle,\;g^{\sharp}\left(\langle\omega,1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle\right)\subset\langle 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},(1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1})^{-}\rangle,

(70)

or

g^{\sharp}\left({\langle-1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},0\rangle}\right)\subset\langle-(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-},2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle,\;g^{\sharp}\left(\langle\omega,1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle\right)\subset\langle(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{+},(1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1})^{-}\rangle.

(71)

with

\displaystyle g(x)\leq x\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}\quad\text{if}\quad 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\leq x\in\mathbb{F}.

(72)

Furthermore, if
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}
, there exist
g_{1}
and
g_{2}
such that they satisfy
Equation

70
and
Equation

71
, respectively. Hence we pick
g
as
g=g_{2}
if
\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0
and
g=g_{1}
if
\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1
.

If
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}
, by
Lemma

24
, there exists
\beta\in\mathbb{F}
such that the following inequality holds:

\frac{1}{2}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}<\beta\otimes K_{\sigma}<\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}.

Therefore we have

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\leq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\oplus\left(\beta\otimes K_{\sigma}\right)\leq(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{+},\;-\left((2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{+}\right)\leq-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\oplus\left(\beta\otimes K_{\sigma}\right)\leq-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.

(73)

Define
\tilde{\beta}\in\mathbb{F}
as

\tilde{\beta}\coloneq\begin{cases}0&\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}.\\
\beta&\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0\text{ and \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:case_psudo_inverse} holds},\\
0&\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1\text{ and \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:case_psudo_inverse} holds},\\
0&\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0\text{ and \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:case_true_inverse} holds},\\
-\beta&\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1\text{ and \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{eq:case_true_inverse}
holds}.\end{cases}

(74)

Define
f(x):\mathbb{F}\rightarrow\mathbb{F}
as

f(x)\coloneq\left({\theta\otimes x}\right)\oplus\left({-\theta\otimes\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{n}\left({\widetilde{\alpha}_{i}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus\left({\tilde{\beta}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus\eta.

Now we analyze the abstract interval arithmetic of
f
.
First, consider the function
\tilde{g}
defined as

\tilde{g}(x)\coloneq g(x)\oplus\left({\tilde{\beta}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus\eta=x\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{n}\left({\widetilde{\alpha_{i}}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus\left({\tilde{\beta}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus\eta.

(75)

Together with
Equations

68
,
69
,
73
and
74
, we have

\tilde{g}^{\sharp}\langle-1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},0\rangle\subset\\
\begin{cases}\langle-(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-},2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta,\eta\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}\text{ and }\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0,\\
\langle-(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-},(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta,\eta\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}\text{ and }\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1,\\
\langle-(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-},(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\left({\tilde{\beta}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta,\eta\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0\text{ and \eqref{eq:case_psudo_inverse} holds},\\
\langle-(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-},(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta,\eta\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1\text{ and \eqref{eq:case_psudo_inverse} holds},\\
\langle-(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-},2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta,\eta\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0\text{ and \eqref{eq:case_true_inverse} holds},\\
\langle-(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{-},2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\left({-\tilde{\beta}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta,\eta\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1\text{ and \eqref{eq:case_true_inverse} holds},\end{cases}

and thus, we have
g^{\prime}\langle-1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},0\rangle=\langle\eta,\eta\rangle
.
Similarly, together with
Equations

67
,
68
,
69
,
73
and
74
, we have
\tilde{g}^{\sharp}\left({\langle\omega,1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle}\right)=\langle\eta^{+},\eta^{+}\rangle
by the following argument:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\tilde{g}^{\sharp}\left({\langle\omega,1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle}\right)\\
\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\subset\begin{cases}\langle(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{+},(1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta^{+},\eta^{+}\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}\text{ and }\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0,\\
\langle 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},(1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta^{+},\eta^{+}\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}\text{ and }\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1,\\
\langle 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},(1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\left({\tilde{\beta}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta^{+},\eta^{+}\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0\text{ and \eqref{eq:case_psudo_inverse} holds},\\
\langle 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},(1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta^{+},\eta^{+}\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1\text{ and \eqref{eq:case_psudo_inverse} holds},\\
\langle(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{+},(1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta^{+},\eta^{+}\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=0\text{ and \eqref{eq:case_true_inverse} holds},\\
\langle(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}})^{+},(1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1})^{-}\rangle\oplus^{\sharp}\left({-\tilde{\beta}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus^{\sharp}\eta&\subset\langle\eta^{+},\eta^{+}\rangle\text{ if }\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1,\mathfrak{s}_{\eta,M}=1\text{ and \eqref{eq:case_true_inverse} holds}.\end{cases}

Now we define
f(x)
and
h(x)
as

\displaystyle h(x)

\displaystyle\coloneq\begin{cases}(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes x)\oplus(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma(\eta))\quad&\text{if}\quad\sigma(\eta)<\sigma(\eta^{+}),\\
(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes x)\oplus(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma(\eta))\quad&\text{if}\quad\sigma(\eta)>\sigma(\eta^{+}),\end{cases}

\displaystyle f(x)

\displaystyle\coloneq h(x)\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{n}\left({\widetilde{\alpha}_{i}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus\left({\tilde{\beta}\otimes K_{\sigma}}\right)\oplus\eta,

We need to show the followings to show
Equation

62
.

\displaystyle h^{\sharp}\left(\mathcal{I}\right)

\displaystyle\subset\langle-1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},0\rangle,

(76)

\displaystyle h^{\sharp}\left(\mathcal{I}^{+}\right)

\displaystyle\subset\langle\omega,1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\rangle.

(77)

To show this, we consider the following cases.

Case 1:
\sigma(\eta)<\sigma(\eta^{+})
.

In this case, we need to show

\displaystyle\begin{cases}h\left(\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\right)\geq-1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\\
h\left(\sigma(\eta)\right)\leq 0\\
h\left(\sigma(\eta^{+})\right)\geq\omega\\
h\left(\sigma(\eta^{+})+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\right)\leq 1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.\end{cases}.

(78)

First, note that for any
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
, unless
2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma
is subnormal,
2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma

is exact. Hence we have

\displaystyle|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times\gamma|\leq\frac{1}{2}\omega.

For
x=\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathrm{rnd}{({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes x})}_{\mathbb{F}}\right|\leq\left|\mathrm{rnd}{({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times x+\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\left|\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\right|\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}+\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\leq 1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle h(x)=\left(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes x\right)\oplus\left(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma(\eta)\right)\geq-1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.

For
x=\sigma\left({\eta}\right)
, we have

h\left(\sigma(\eta)\right)=\left(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma\left({\eta}\right)\right)\oplus\left(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma(\eta)\right)=0.

For
x=\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)
, by
Lemma

22
, since
\left|\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)\right|\geq 2^{e_{0}}
and
-e_{0}-M+\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1=\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}\in\mathbb{F}
, we have

h\left(\sigma(\eta^{+})\right)=\left(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)\right)\oplus\left(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)\right)\geq\omega.

For
x=\sigma(\eta^{+})+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}
, similar to the case of
x=\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}
, we have

\displaystyle h(x)=\left(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes x\right)\oplus\left(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma(\eta)\right)\leq 1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.

Therefore, due to
Equation

78
,
Equations

76
and
77
hold.

Additionally, we need to show
Equations

63
and
64
.

For
x>\sigma(\eta)+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\theta\otimes x\right)\oplus\left(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma\left({\eta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{rnd}{({\theta\otimes x-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma\left({\eta}\right)})}_{\mathbb{F}}\geq\mathrm{rnd}{({x\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}-\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}-\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}

\displaystyle\geq\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({\left(\sigma(\eta)+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\right)^{+}-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}-\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\geq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.

Hence, by
Equation

72
, we have

g\left(\left(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes x\right)\oplus\left(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\sigma(\eta)\right)\right)\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle f(x)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}\oplus\left(\beta\otimes K_{\sigma}\right)\oplus\eta

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}\oplus\left(\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}\right)\oplus\eta

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}+e_{0}+1}+\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}\oplus\left(\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}\right)\oplus\eta

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}})}_{\mathbb{F}}\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}\oplus\left(\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}\right)\oplus\eta.

where we use
2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\geq 8\omega
.

If
\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\leq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-1}
, since

\displaystyle\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}+\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}\oplus\left(\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}\right)<1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1},

we have
f(x)\leq\eta^{+}
.

If
\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}>2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-1}
, there exist
k\in\mathbb{N}
such that
k\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-1}\leq\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}<(k+1)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-1}
. Then,

\displaystyle f(x)

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}})}_{\mathbb{F}}\oplus\left({2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}}\right)^{+}\oplus\left(\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}\right)\oplus\eta

\displaystyle\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta+1}}+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}})}_{\mathbb{F}}\oplus\left(\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-M}\right)\oplus\eta

\displaystyle\leq\eta^{+}+\left\lceil{\frac{k+1}{4}}\right\rceil_{\mathbb{Z}}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}+1}\leq\eta^{+}+4\times\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta^{+}}\right)}\right)\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}.

Similarly, for
x<\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}
we have

\eta-f(x)\leq\left({x-\sigma\left({\eta}\right)}\right)\times 4\theta.

Case 2:
\sigma(\eta)>\sigma(\eta^{+})
.

In this case, we need to show

\displaystyle\begin{cases}h\left(\sigma(\eta)-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\right)\leq 1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\\
h\left(\sigma(\eta^{+})\right)\geq\omega\\
h\left(\sigma(\eta)\right)\leq 0\\
h\left(\sigma(\eta^{+})+2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\right)\geq-1.1\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.\end{cases}.

(79)

By similar arguments to
Case 1
, we can show
Equation

79
and
Equations

65
and
66
.
∎

0.F.3
Technical Lemmas for Lemma
5

This subsection presents technical lemmas for the proof of
Lemma

5
(
Section

0.D.2
).

Lemma 18

Let
x\in\mathbb{F}
be normal. Then for any
c\in\mathbb{F}
such that
\mathfrak{e}_{c}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}
and
|\mathfrak{e}_{x}-\mathfrak{e}_{c}|\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\max}
, there exists
w\in\mathbb{F}
such that

\displaystyle w\otimes x=c\pm 2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{c}}.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we assume
x,c>0
and
\mathfrak{e}_{x}=1
. For any normal
a=1.\mathfrak{s}_{a,1}\dots\mathfrak{s}_{a,M}
, we have

\displaystyle(a^{+}\otimes x)-(a\otimes x)=\mathrm{rnd}{({a^{+}\otimes x})}-\mathrm{rnd}{({a\otimes x})}=2^{-M}\;\text{or}\;2^{1-M}.

since
(a^{+}\times x)-(a\times x)=2^{-M}\times x<2^{1-M}
. Since the gap is
2^{-M}
or
2^{1-M}
, we can pick
w\in\mathbb{F}
such that
w\otimes x=c\pm 2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{c}}
.
∎

Lemma 19

(Floating-point distributive law)

Let
a,b,c\in\mathbb{F}
be normal. Then we have

\displaystyle a\otimes(b\oplus c)=(a\otimes b)+(a\otimes c)+(C\times 2^{-M-1}),

where
|C|\leq|a|\left((2+2^{-M-1})|b+c|+|b|+|c|\right)
.

Proof.

Since

\displaystyle b\oplus c=(b+c)(1+\delta_{1}),\quad|\delta_{1}|\leq\varepsilon=2^{-M-1}.

We have

\displaystyle a\otimes(b\oplus c)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{rnd}{({a\times\mathrm{rnd}{({b+c})}})}=\left(a\times(b+c)\right)(1+\delta_{1})(1+\delta_{2})

\displaystyle=\left(ab+ac\right)(1+\delta_{1})(1+\delta_{2}),\quad|\delta_{1}|,|\delta_{2}|\leq\varepsilon.

Since

\displaystyle(a\otimes b)+(a\otimes c)=ab(1+\delta_{3})+ac(1+\delta_{4})=a(b+c)+a(b\delta_{3}+c\delta_{4}),\quad|\delta_{3}|,|\delta_{4}|\leq\varepsilon

the difference between
a\otimes(b\oplus c)
and
(a\otimes b)+(a\otimes c)
is

\displaystyle a\otimes(b\oplus c)-\left((a\otimes b)+(a\otimes c)\right)

\displaystyle=(ab+ac)\left(\delta_{1}+\delta_{2}+\delta_{1}\delta_{2}\right)-a(b\delta_{3}+c\delta_{4})

Therefore,

\displaystyle|a\otimes(b\oplus c)-\left((a\otimes b)+(a\otimes c)\right)|\leq|a|\left(|b+c|(2\varepsilon+\varepsilon^{2})+(|b|+|c|)\varepsilon\right).

∎

Lemma 20

Let
x,y\in\mathbb{F}
. Suppose
x=0
or
\mathfrak{e}_{x}\leq-3-2M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}
where
\mathfrak{e}_{y}\geq 1+\mathfrak{e}_{\min}
. Then
for
a\in\{-2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}},2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}\}
, there exist
\alpha_{1},\dots,\alpha_{3}\in\mathbb{F}
such that

\displaystyle x\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.02228pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{3}\alpha_{i}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle y\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.02228pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{3}\alpha_{i}

\displaystyle=y+a.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we assume
y>0
.

Case (1)

\mathfrak{s}_{y,M}=0
.

If
a=-2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
, let
\alpha_{1}=\alpha_{2}=2^{-1-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
,
\alpha_{3}=-2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
.

If
a=2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
, let
\alpha_{1}=\alpha_{2}=-2^{-1-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
,
\alpha_{3}=2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
.

Case (2)

\mathfrak{s}_{y,M}=1
.

If
a=-2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
, let
\alpha_{1}=-2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
,
\alpha_{2}=\alpha_{3}=2^{-1-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
.

If
a=2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
, let
\alpha_{1}=2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
,
\alpha_{2}=\alpha_{3}=-2^{-1-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
.

∎

Lemma 21

Let
x,y\in\mathbb{F}
. Suppose
x=0
or
\mathfrak{e}_{x}\leq-4-2M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}
where
\mathfrak{e}_{y}\geq 1+\mathfrak{e}_{\min}
. Then
for
a\in\{-2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}},2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}\}
, there exist
\alpha_{1},\dots,\alpha_{5}\in\mathbb{F}
of the form
\alpha_{i}=1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\alpha_{i}}}
such that

\displaystyle x\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.02228pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{5}\alpha_{i}

\displaystyle=0,

\displaystyle y\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.02228pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{5}\alpha_{i}

\displaystyle=y+a.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we assume
y>0
.

First, note that

\displaystyle 1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}\oplus 1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}

\displaystyle=11.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M-1\text{ times }},

\displaystyle 11.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M-1\text{ times }}\oplus 1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{rnd}{({101.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M-2\text{ times }}01})}=101.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M-2\text{ times }},

\displaystyle 101.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M-2\text{ times }}\oplus 1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{rnd}{({111.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M-3\text{ times }}011})}=111.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M-2\text{ times }}.

Hence
\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{4}1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}=1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}\times 2^{2}
.

Therefore, if
a=\pm 2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}
, let

\displaystyle\alpha_{1}=\alpha_{2}=\mp 1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}\times 2^{-2-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}},\alpha_{3}=\pm 1.\underbrace{1\dots 1}_{M\text{ times }}\times 2^{-1-M+\mathfrak{e}_{y}}.

Then we have

\displaystyle x\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{3}\alpha_{i}

\displaystyle=0,\quad y\oplus\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{3}\alpha_{i}=y+a.

∎

Lemma 22

Consider normal floating-point numbers
\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\in\mathbb{F}
with
\mathfrak{e}_{\gamma_{1}}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\gamma_{2}}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1
. Suppose an integer
e_{0}\in\mathbb{Z}
satisfies the following:

2^{e_{0}}\leq|\gamma_{1}-\gamma_{2}|<2^{e_{0}+1}.

Define
\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}
as

\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}\coloneq\max\left({\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M,-e_{0}+\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1}\right).

Then, we have

\left(2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{1}\right)\oplus\left(-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{2}\right)\neq 0.

Proof.

For any
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
, unless
2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma
is subnormal ,
2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma
is exact. Hence,

\displaystyle\left|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{1}-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times\gamma_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\omega,

\displaystyle\left|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{2}-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times\gamma_{2}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\omega.

Since

e_{0}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\gamma_{1}}-M-1
and
e_{0}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\gamma_{2}}-M-1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}+\mathfrak{e}_{\gamma_{1}}=-e_{0}+\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1+\mathfrak{e}_{\gamma_{1}}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}

Therefore following inequalities hold:

\displaystyle\left|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{1}-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{2}\right|

\displaystyle\geq\left|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times\gamma_{1}-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times\gamma_{2}\right|-\left|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{1}-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times\gamma_{1}\right|-\left|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{2}-2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\times\gamma_{2}\right|

\displaystyle\geq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1}-\omega=\omega.

Since
|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{1}|,|2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\theta}}\otimes\gamma_{2}|\leq 2^{1+\mathfrak{e}_{\min}}
, their gap to adjacent number is
\omega
. Therefore they are distinct.
∎

0.F.4
Technical Lemma for Lemma
6

This subsection presents technical lemma for the proof of
Lemma

6
(
Section

0.D.3
).

Lemma 23

For
1\leq x<1+2^{-1}
, we have
(1+2^{-M})\otimes x=x^{+}
. For
1+2^{-1}\leq x\leq 2-2^{-1-M}
, we have
(1+2^{-M})\otimes x=x^{++}
. For
x=2-2^{-M}
we have
(1+2^{-M})\otimes x=x^{+}
.

Proof.

(2^{-1}+2^{-1-M})\otimes x=\mathrm{rnd}{({1+2^{-1-M}-2^{-1-2M}})}=1.

∎

0.F.5
Technical Lemmas for §
0.E

This subsection presents technical lemma for the proofs in
Appendix

0.E
.

Lemma 24

Let
K\in\mathbb{F}
with
|K|\in[(1+2^{-M+1})\times 2^{-M-2},1+2^{-2}-2^{-M}]_{\mathbb{F}}
.
Consider
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\in\mathbb{Z}
such that
\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\max}-M
.
Then, there exists
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
such that the following inequality holds:

\frac{1}{2}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}<\gamma\otimes K\leq\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.

Proof.

Let
K
be represented as

K=\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{K}},\quad-M-2\leq\mathfrak{e}_{K}\leq 0.

We consider the following cases.

Case 1:
\mathfrak{e}_{\max}-M-1\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\max}-M
.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in[1,1+2^{-M}]_{\mathbb{F}}
, we have
-M-1\leq\mathfrak{e}_{K}\leq 0
.
We define
\gamma
as

\displaystyle\gamma\coloneq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1}.

Since
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\max}-M-(M-1)-1=\mathfrak{e}_{\max}
, we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.
Then,

\displaystyle 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-1}\leq\gamma\times K\leq(1+2^{-M})\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-1}.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in[1+2^{-M+1},2)_{\mathbb{F}}
,
we define
\gamma
as

\gamma\coloneq(2-2^{-M})\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-2}.

Since
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-2\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\max}-M-(-M-2)-2=\mathfrak{e}_{\max}
, we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.

Because

\displaystyle(2-2^{-M})\times(1+2^{-M+1})=2+3\times 2^{-M}-2^{-2M+1}

\displaystyle>2,

\displaystyle(2-2^{-M})\times(2-2^{-M})=2^{2}-2^{2-M}+2^{-2M}

\displaystyle<4,

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}<\gamma\otimes K<2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}.

Case 2:
\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+2\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\max}-M-2
.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in\left[1,1+2^{-2}\right]_{\mathbb{F}}
, define
\gamma
as

\gamma\coloneq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}}.

As
\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+2\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\max}
, we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.
Then, we have

2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}\leq\gamma\otimes K=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}})}_{\mathbb{F}}\leq\mathrm{rnd}{({\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}})}_{\mathbb{F}}=\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},

where the last equality is followed by
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+2
.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in\left(1+2^{-2},2\right)_{\mathbb{F}}
, define
\gamma
as

\gamma\coloneq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1}.

As
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1
, we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.
Then, we have

\frac{1}{2}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}}<\gamma\otimes K=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-1}})}_{\mathbb{F}}\leq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},

where the first inequality is followed from
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-1\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1
and
\mathfrak{s}_{K}>1+2^{-2}
.

Case 3:
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}=\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1
.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in[1,\frac{5}{4})
, define
\gamma
as

\gamma\coloneq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}}.

Since
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1
, we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.
Then,

\gamma\otimes K=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1}})}_{\mathbb{F}}=2\omega=2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},

since
2\omega\leq\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1}<\frac{5}{2}\omega
.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in\left[\frac{5}{4},\frac{5}{3}\right)
, define
\gamma
as

\gamma\coloneq(1+2^{-1})\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1}.

As
\mathfrak{e}_{K}\leq-1
,
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M+1
, and we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.
Then,

\gamma\otimes K=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times(1+2^{-1})\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M}})}_{\mathbb{F}}=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times(1+2^{-1})\times\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}=2\omega=2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},

since
\frac{3}{2}\omega<\frac{15}{8}\omega\leq\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times(1+2^{-1})\times\omega<\frac{5}{2}\omega
.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in\left({\frac{5}{3},2}\right)
, define
\gamma
as

\gamma\coloneq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1}.

Since
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M
, we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.
Then,

\gamma\otimes K=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M}})}_{\mathbb{F}}=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}=2\omega=2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},

since
\frac{3}{2}\omega<\frac{5}{3}\omega\leq\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times\omega<2\omega
.

Case 4:
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}=\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M
.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in[1,\frac{3}{2})
, define
\gamma
as

\gamma\coloneq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}}.

Since
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M
, we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.
Then,

\gamma\otimes K=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M}})}_{\mathbb{F}}=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}=\omega=2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},

since
\omega\leq\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times\omega<\frac{3}{2}\omega
.

If
\mathfrak{s}_{K}\in(\frac{3}{2},2)
, we have
\mathfrak{e}_{K}\leq-1
by the assumption.
Define
\gamma
as

\gamma\coloneq 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1}.

Since
\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}-\mathfrak{e}_{K}-1\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M
, we have
\gamma\in\mathbb{F}
.
Then,

\gamma\otimes K=\mathrm{rnd}{({\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\min}-M-1}})}_{\mathbb{F}}=\mathrm{rnd}{({\frac{1}{2}\times\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times\omega})}_{\mathbb{F}}=\omega=2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\zeta}},

since
\frac{1}{2}\omega<\frac{3}{4}\omega\leq\frac{1}{2}\times\mathfrak{s}_{K}\times\omega<\omega
. This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 25

Let
0=z_{0}<z_{1}<\cdots<z_{(|\mathbb{F}|-1)/2}=\Omega<z_{(|\mathbb{F}|+1)/2}=\infty
be all non-negative floats in
\mathbb{F}
.
Then, for any
j\in[(|\mathbb{F}|+1)/2]\cup\{0\}
and
x_{i}\in(2^{-1}\times(z_{i}-z_{i-1}),(1+2^{-1})\times(z_{i}-z_{i-1}))_{\mathbb{F}}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.02228pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{j}x_{i}=z_{j}.

Proof.

Since it is obvious for
j=0
we consider
j\geq 1
, and we use the mathematical induction on
j
.

Base step:
j=1
.

For the base step, note that
z_{1}=\omega
. Then we have

x_{1}\in(2^{-1}\times(z_{1}-z_{0}),(1+2^{-1})\times(z_{1}-z_{0}))_{\mathbb{F}}=(\frac{1}{2}\times\omega,\frac{3}{2}\times\omega)_{\mathbb{F}}=\{\omega\}=\{z_{1}\}.

Induction step.

Assume that
\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{j}x_{i}=z_{j}
(inductive hypothesis). We write
z_{j}
as
\mathfrak{s}_{z_{j}}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{z_{j}}}
.
We consider the following cases.

Case 1:
2^{\mathfrak{e}_{z_{j}}}\leq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+1
.

In this case,
z_{j+1}-z_{j}=\omega
.
Consider the case of
j+1
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{j+1}x_{i}

\displaystyle=\left({\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{j}x_{i}}\right)\oplus x_{j+1}=z_{j}\oplus x_{j+1}=z_{j+1},

where the last equality follows from

\displaystyle x_{j+1}

\displaystyle\in\left(2^{-1}\times(z_{j+1}-z_{j}),(1+2^{-1})\times(z_{j+1}-z_{j})\right)_{\mathbb{F}}=(\frac{1}{2}\times\omega,\frac{3}{2}\times\omega)_{\mathbb{F}}=\{\omega\}.

Case 2:
2^{\mathfrak{e}_{z_{j}}}\geq\mathfrak{e}_{\min}+2
.

In this case,
z_{j+1}-z_{j}=2^{-M+\mathfrak{e}_{z_{j}}}
Consider the case of
j+1
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{j+1}x_{i}

\displaystyle=\left({\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.00006pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{j}x_{i}}\right)\oplus x_{j+1}=z_{j}\oplus x_{j+1}=z_{j+1},

where the last equality follows from

\displaystyle x_{j+1}

\displaystyle\in\left(2^{-1}\times(z_{j+1}-z_{j}),(1+2^{-1})\times(z_{j+1}-z_{j})\right)_{\mathbb{F}}

\displaystyle=\left(2^{-M-1+\mathfrak{e}_{x_{j}}},(1+2^{-1})\times 2^{-M-1+\mathfrak{e}_{x_{j}}}\right)_{\mathbb{F}}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 26

For any
\eta\in[-2^{3},2^{3})_{\mathbb{F}}
,
x\in[(1+2^{-p+1})\times 2^{-p-2},1+2^{-1}-2^{-p}]_{\mathbb{F}}
, and
n\in[2^{p}]
, there exist
k\in\mathbb{N}
and
\alpha,\beta,z_{1},\dots,z_{k},\theta_{1},\dots,\theta_{k}\in\mathbb{F}
such that

\displaystyle\left({\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.02228pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{n}\alpha\otimes x}\right)\oplus(\theta_{1}\otimes\sigma(z_{1}))\oplus\cdots\oplus(\theta_{k}\otimes\sigma(z_{k}))\oplus\beta\in[\eta^{+},\infty)_{\mathbb{F}},

\displaystyle\left({\mathop{\mathchoice{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 1.02228pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\circ$}}\hss}{\sum}}{\vbox{\hbox to0.0pt{\kern 0.0pt\raisebox{-2.55554pt}{\scalebox{2.0}{$\scriptstyle\circ$}}\hss}}{\sum}}{\sum}{\sum}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{n-1}\alpha\otimes x}\right)\oplus(\theta_{1}\otimes\sigma(z_{1}))\oplus\cdots\oplus(\theta_{k}\otimes\sigma(z_{k}))\oplus\beta\in(-\infty,\eta]_{\mathbb{F}}.

Proof.

If
\eta
is normal, by
Lemma

24
, there exists
\alpha\in\mathbb{F}
such that the following inequality holds:
if
\eta>0
,

\frac{1}{2}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}-M}\leq\alpha\otimes x<\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}-M}.

if
\eta<0
,

\frac{1}{2}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}-M-1}\leq\alpha\otimes x<\frac{5}{4}\times 2^{\mathfrak{e}_{\eta}-M-1}.

Then, for
n=1
,

\eta\oplus\left({\sum_{i=1}^{n}\alpha\otimes x}\right)=\eta\oplus\left({\alpha\otimes x}\right)=\eta^{+}.

(80)

If
\eta
is subnormal, consider
\alpha\in\mathbb{F}
such that
\alpha\otimes x=\omega
.
Then, for
n=1
,

\eta\oplus\left({\sum_{i=1}^{n}\alpha\otimes x}\right)=\eta\oplus\omega=\eta^{+}.

(81)

This completes the proof.
∎
